| Where | Tool / rule | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| [`zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/DlmmMath.sol:131`](../zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/DlmmMath.sol#L131) | aderyn / incorrect-caret-operator | **false-positive** (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, initial review by reading the code), 2026-10-30): intentional XOR, not exponentiation: this is the 512-bit mulDiv (Remco Bloemen / Uniswap FullMath). (3*d)^2 seeds the Newton iteration for the inverse of the odd d mod 2^256 with 4 correct bits; the six "inv *= 2 - d * inv" steps that follow double that to 256. <br>`aderyn:incorrect-caret-operator:zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/DlmmMath.sol:3f59e3ab82c8` |
| [`zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/ZetaphyDlmmFactory.sol:135`](../zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/ZetaphyDlmmFactory.sol#L135) | aderyn / reentrancy-state-change | **false-positive** (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, auditor triage of the open Highs, by reading the code), 2026-10-30): createPair: pairDeployer is immutable, created by this factory's constructor (`new ZetaphyDlmmPairDeployer()`), and its deploy() only CREATE2s a ZetaphyDlmmPair (whose constructor makes one view call back for its parameters); no token or user code runs. getPair is checked empty before, and the CREATE2 salt (tokenX, tokenY, binStep) makes a second pair for the same key impossible. <br>`aderyn:reentrancy-state-change:zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/ZetaphyDlmmFactory.sol:90694f24e172` |

| [`zetaphy-network/contracts/NeuroJobs.sol:867`](../zetaphy-network/contracts/NeuroJobs.sol#L867) | aderyn / weak-randomness | **accepted-risk** (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, auditor triage of the open Highs, by reading the code), 2026-10-30): j.seed (requester, chain, target, timestamp, counter) is only a per-job salt, not the randomness: runner selection uses keccak256(seed, blockhash(startBlock + 1), round), a block after the request/round opening, so neither the requester nor a runner retrying can pick it (contract docs, "Lottery bound to a block nobody picks"). Remaining, documented weakness: the proposer of block startBlock + 1 can grind that block's hash (validator-level bias), and on a chain whose BLOCKHASH is 0 the fallback is as predictable as the request. The chain's own randomness (PREVRANDAO from the PQ vote certificate, zetaphy-chain README item 8) is also proposer-biasable, so it would not remove the bias. Accepted for testnet; mainnet stakes on the lottery need commit-reveal or a VRF/beacon. <br>`aderyn:weak-randomness:zetaphy-network/contracts/NeuroJobs.sol:6e5cf1246faa` |

### Fixed (no longer detected)

Findings `triage.yml` records as fixed whose tool no longer reports them: the record of the fix. A fixed finding that comes back shows as open again.

- `aderyn:unsafe-casting:zetaphy-network/contracts/NeuroScore.sol:4e47b7e79173` — NeuroScore._score stored int64(score) unchecked. Fixed in 6829bee: _toInt64 reverts ScoreOutOfRange outside int64 instead of storing a truncated score (the event uses the same value); regression test "a score outside int64 is refused" in zetaphy-network/test/neuroscore.test.js. (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, auditor triage of the open Highs, by reading the code), 2026-10-30)
- `aderyn:unsafe-casting:zetaphy-network/contracts/NeuroScore.sol:17b11df7e29a` — NeuroScore._score emitted int64(score) unchecked. Fixed in 6829bee: the event now carries the range-checked value from _toInt64 (reverts ScoreOutOfRange); regression test in zetaphy-network/test/neuroscore.test.js. (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, auditor triage of the open Highs, by reading the code), 2026-10-30)
- `osv-scanner:GHSA-3gc7-fjrx-p6mg:yarn.lock:035bd1566304` — bigint-buffer@1.1.5 (native toBigInt buffer overflow, no fixed release) is replaced in 6829bee by scripts/vendor/bigint-buffer, a pure-JS drop-in with the same four functions, through the root package.json resolutions; yarn.lock no longer has bigint-buffer@1.1.5, bindings or file-uri-to-path. Tests: scripts/vendor/bigint-buffer/test.js; @solana/spl-token layouts decode/encode through it. (pospos191 (owner, confirmed 2026-10-30; proposed by claude-agent, auditor triage of the open Highs, by reading the code), 2026-10-30)

## Formal checks (SMTChecker)

| Contract file | Engine | Result | Counterexamples | Unproved targets | Time |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/ZetaphyCheques.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 11 | 504s |
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/RewardVault.sol` | chc | done | 2 | 36 | 614s |
| `zetaphy-dex/contracts/WZETAPHY.sol` | chc | done | 1 | 0 | 20s |
| `zetaphy-dex/contracts/ZetaphyPair.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 47 | 306s |
| `zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/DlmmMath.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 0 | 1s |
| `zetaphy-dex/contracts/dlmm/ZetaphyDlmmPair.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 125 | 303s |
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/HyperlaneLockBoxV2.sol` | chc | time-budget | 0 | 0 | 900s |
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/HackInsurance.sol` | chc | time-budget | 0 | 0 | 900s |
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/StableGateway.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 16 | 484s |
| `zetaphy-network/contracts/ZetaphyOfflineOrders.sol` | chc | done | 0 | 12 | 486s |
| `zetaphy-nft/contracts/NFTMarketplace.sol` | chc | out-of-memory-or-crash | 0 | 0 | 189s |

A counterexample is a concrete input the solver found (BMC checks each function alone, without the contract's state invariants, so its arithmetic ones are often unreachable; they are Low findings). An unproved target means the solver ran out of time: NOT a pass and NOT a bug (Info). A contract that hit the time or memory cap reports nothing.

The project test suites run contracts on an in-process EVM through `node --test` helpers, which solidity-coverage and `forge coverage` cannot instrument; the mutation score above is the measure of test strength used instead. Suites under `*/audit/invariants/` that use Foundry can add `forge coverage` later.

## Neuro on our own bytecode (dogfooding)

Neuro read 148 of our contracts (offline, bytecode only): 120 low, 7 info, 19 high, 2 medium. This is what users of the explorer and wallet would see. A high verdict names a power (pause, mint, an owner that can move funds): it must be documented for users, or it is a Neuro false positive to fix in Neuro. Not counted as vulnerabilities.

| Contract | Neuro verdict | What Neuro reports |
|---|---|---|
| NeuroLane (`zetaphy-network/contracts/BridgeAirlock.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-pause:high |
| BridgeGuard (`zetaphy-network/contracts/BridgeGuard.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-mint:high, can-pause:high, delegatecall:medium |
| BridgedToken (`zetaphy-network/contracts/BridgeGuard.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-mint:high |
| BugBounty (`zetaphy-network/contracts/BugBounty.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-pause:high |
| FederatedRounds (`zetaphy-network/contracts/FederatedRounds.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-pause:high |
| HyperlaneLockBoxV2 (`zetaphy-network/contracts/HyperlaneLockBoxV2.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-pause:high |
| ImmuneMirror (`zetaphy-network/contracts/ImmuneMirror.sol`) | high | owner-unknown:medium, can-pause:high |
| ImmuneVaccines (`zetaphy-network/contracts/ImmuneVaccines.sol`) | high | selfdestruct:high |

## What this does NOT prove (as of 2026-11-10)

- **No finding is not "secure".** Static analysers know patterns; they miss business-logic bugs (wrong fee maths, a price that can be pushed, a missing check that only the design says must exist), which is where most money is lost.
- **Economic and cross-contract attacks are not modelled**: flash-loan price manipulation, oracle manipulation, MEV / sandwiching, governance capture, griefing that costs the attacker little.
- **The SMTChecker proves only what it reports as proved**, within its time budget, and only the built-in properties (assert, arithmetic, bounds). "Unproved" means unknown.
- **Fuzzing and invariants** test only the properties someone wrote down, for the number of runs given. Suites that are missing are counted as "not run", never as passed.
- **Mutation testing** samples a few dozen mutants of a few contracts; a high score means the tests notice those kinds of edits, not that they are complete.
- **Deployment and operations are out of scope**: who holds the owner / admin keys, the multisig and timelock settings actually deployed, constructor arguments, upgrade procedures, the RPC and front-ends. The code hashes above let you check that what is deployed is what was analysed.
- **The chain**: only the ZETAPHY patches are analysed, not Cosmos EVM / CometBFT / ibc-go themselves, and consensus-level behaviour (liveness, validator collusion) is not tested here.
- **Tools have bugs and blind spots**, and the versions used are pinned (audit/tools/install.sh); a newer rule set may find more.
- **Before mainnet with real funds, an independent audit by people is still required** (SECURITY.md). This pipeline makes that audit cheaper and keeps regressions out between audits; it does not replace it.
